| 2026-08-22-optimizer-catalog-honesty | 2026-08-22 | [advisory] (DECISION RECORD + EXECUTION — executes the decision-maker's Aug 22 ruling on the optimizer audit: "the AI should see provider/expert offerings; proceed with the recommendation". Fixes audit findings 1+3+4; finding 2 is FILED for a ruling, deliberately NOT fixed.) | **THE OPTIMIZER'S CATALOG IS APPROVED-ONLY AND DESTINATION-SCOPED; VARIANT ITEMS CARRY REAL COORDINATES (transport legs go live).** The Aug 22 optimizer audit found the two optimizer entry points pulled an arbitrary, unordered 100 rows of `provider_services` filtered on `status='active'` ONLY — no `approval_status` gate (an F2 read-side-gate violation: born-`submitted` listings could be offered to the AI, land in traveler-facing variants, and surface as upsells) and no market scoping (a Kyoto trip could be offered mostly other cities' inventory — so in practice the AI often optimized without the catalog at all, the exact complaint the decision-maker raised). Landed: **(a)** ONE shared `loadOptimizerCatalog(destination)` (`optimizer-baseline.service.ts`, the L6 home both entry points already share for baseline loading) — `active` + `approved` + `location ILIKE city|destination` (the upsell query's existing precedent); zero city matches ⇒ the AI is offered NO catalog rows and may only propose free-text activities (`providerServiceId` NULL) — honest emptiness, never another city's services (§13); null destination ⇒ unscoped active+approved (no invented city). Both inline pulls in `routes.ts` replaced; the upsell query gains the same `approved` filter; the stale in-code comment claiming an approved query ("already the caller's approved-services query") corrected to cite reality. **(b) Coordinates:** the audit found `calculateTransportLegs` was WIRED BUT DEAD — gated on variant-item lat/lng that no writer ever set — so every `travel_time` figure was the LLM's self-reported guess and the slip review's drive-time preview chip could never render. Now: the trip-backed baseline loader reads real coordinates (the item's own pin, else the linked catalog row's migration-129 columns) onto baseline items → baseline variant items; AI variant items copy the VALIDATED `providerServiceId`'s catalog coordinates; an AI-invented item with no catalog row stays NULL/NULL — its location is a string the model wrote, and coordinates are never geocoded from a guess (§13). Located variants now get real (heuristic haversine×1.3) legs, and the `2026-08-22-slip-optimize-review-first` drive-time delta computes from them. **(c) FILED, NOT FIXED — decision-maker ruling requested:** `booking.service.ts` (~331-346) resolves a booking price with a fallback read of `itinerary_variant_items.price`, a column whose value is the LLM's own number written verbatim at variant insert — server-side storage, model provenance. §14 as written ("server-side catalog/record") is satisfied textually but not in spirit; the proposed fix (re-derive from the linked catalog row; refuse unlinked) touches the money path and awaits the ruling. Also noted, unbuilt: expert itinerary templates / Ready-Made Trips remain invisible to the optimizer (separate product — a design question, not a query fix); first runs still pass no trip preferences (audit finding 5, unaddressed this lane). tsc unchanged at baseline (159 local / 157 CI); money-endpoints + unmounted-routers clean. No schema/migration change (columns all pre-existing) → no publish-trap surface. | Aug 22 optimizer audit; decision-maker ruling (offerings visibility); F2 read-gate; §13; L6; `optimizer-baseline.service.ts`; `itinerary-optimizer.ts`; `routes.ts`; enables `2026-08-22-slip-optimize-review-first` drive-time chip |

Three gaps closed: **(1) CRITICAL — `refundReadyMadePurchaseLedger` (`ready-made-purchase.service.ts`) deleted the clone UNCONDITIONALLY**, so a buyer who added their own items and booked a real platform service onto the clone during the 7-day escrow, then refunded, had their own `itinerary_items` cascade-destroyed (`trip_id ON DELETE CASCADE`) and their separately-paid `service_booking` orphaned (`trip_id ON DELETE SET NULL`) but never refunded — a live charge on a deleted trip, and the bookings-have-purchased-items invariant broken. The delete is now guarded by the SAME predicate the build-delete path already enforces (`ready-made.routes.ts`: item `routing_status='purchased' OR bookingId IS NOT NULL`): with paid history the clone is PRESERVED (soft-revoke) — the ready-made charge is still refunded and the author earning still reversed, only the destructive delete is withheld; with no paid history the original revoke (delete) stands. Proven by two negatives in `refund-retry-convergence.test.ts` (preserve-on-paid-history, delete-on-none). This also anticipates the ratified concierge-revision model (below), under which the clone is never destroyed. **(2) MEDIUM — the trip-level `expertTravelerNote` (§21, "Note from your expert") was dropped at clone time** — the buyer never received the traveler-facing note they paid for. The clone now reads it from the SOURCE trip and carries it onto the clone; the PRIVATE `expertNotes` build-notes field is still deliberately NOT copied (§21 — never a traveler surface); per-item `expertNote` already rode along via the `...rest` copy. **(3) LOW — `itinerary_items.origin` was left NULL on manually-added items** (the create handler passed `parsed.data` straight through; only PATCH stripped/omitted it). §12 requires server-side stamping from the actor: an owner's manual add is now `'traveler'`, an assigned advisor/expert's is `'expert'` — stamped from `tripRole`, never `req.body`. **RATIFIED MODEL (mock to follow, NOT built here):** ready-made purchases move to "no self-serve refund; one included consultation + one revision from the selling expert" (expert gets advisor WRITE on the buyer's clone via `tripExpertAdvisors`, Suggest→approve flow; escrow window = revision SLA; **admin-mediated dispute refund kept as escape hatch** — a blanket no-refund is legally shaky and traps a buyer if an expert ghosts). The My Bookings query did `res.json()` unconditionally → for the byte path `.json()` threw → the catch returned null → the download button rendered ONLY for legacy pasted URLs, never for a protected upload. Second-order: hitting that streaming GET on page LOAD both downloaded the whole file and wrote a `deliverable_downloads` row — arming the `artifact_timer` completion clock on a mere render, not a real download. Fix: a side-effect-free **metadata probe** `GET /api/service-bookings/:id/deliverable/meta` (same four gates: own booking · confirmed · artifact-delivery · file present; NO byte download, NO log; returns `{available, protected, deliveryMethod, fileUrl?}` — the objstore KEY is never exposed, only the `protected` flag). The client queries the probe; the button downloads on CLICK — a protected file is fetched as a blob from the streaming GET (which now logs a REAL download) and opened via an object URL, a legacy file links to its `fileUrl`. `NO_DELIVERABLE_UPLOADED` renders honest "your expert hasn't uploaded it yet" (§13), never a dead button. **(2) Post-purchase facts.** The operational "Good to know" subset — meeting point, getting there, start window, changes-until, duration, what to bring, access — rendered ONLY on the pre-purchase `/services/:id` page; the buyer lost every one of them at checkout. Now rendered on the confirmed My Bookings card, composed from the SAME `service-good-to-know` formatters the traveler page uses (§18 — wording source of truth, never re-implemented; a purposeful post-purchase SUBSET, not the full nine-fact card) off the service object `/api/service-bookings` already enriches each booking with. §13: an unanswered field is OMITTED, never defaulted. Read LIVE (operational facts are better current than snapshotted — a moved meeting point should update); the per-booking snapshot the audit filed governs price/deliverable and is a separate lane. **FILED, not in this PR (stated negative space, §18d):** the provider-side complete-booking endpoint has no client caller (call/video/voice_notes/async_messaging have no provider-triggerable completion UI); the admin dispute view carries no delivery evidence; the price/deliverable per-booking snapshot — three separate deliverables-lane follow-ups. Client + one additive read-only endpoint; no schema change. tsc unchanged at baseline (159 local / 157 CI); money-endpoints guard green. | decision-maker Q Aug 23; deliverables audit; D3/S9 (ledger 102); §13/§18; `server/routes.ts` (deliverable meta); `my-bookings.tsx`; `service-good-to-know.ts` (reused) |

Mock-first, decision-maker sign-off Aug 23; ships in two lanes, UI first.) | **AFFILIATE IS A THIRD GROUNDING RUNG, BOOKED VIA THE §16 AGENT RAIL.** The Phase-1 resolver gains a rung between catalog and DMO: **catalog → affiliate → DMO → honest suggestion** (ratified Q1). Match source is **both** the persisted `affiliate_products` registry AND live feeds, reconciled: registry first (deterministic), then best-effort live-feed match that is **materialized into `affiliate_products` (upsert by external_id) before it is linked** — so the item's link and its §16 `bookingToken` always point at a real persisted row (ratified Q2; advances the §16 "one content home" consolidation, no second rail). Live-feed calls are **fail-closed** — a slow/failed feed degrades to registry-only and never blocks the build (§13, groundAiItems never throws). Eligibility **honors the row's `bookingType`**: `affiliate_bookable` → agent rail, `in_platform_bookable` → add-to-cart, unclassified → unlinked (ratified Q3). Grounding **lights up per market automatically** — a market with no active affiliate rows grounds nothing on that rung and falls through, no global flag (ratified Q4). The traveler CTA is **"Book via your Traveloure agent"** through the EXISTING `POST /api/affiliate-booking-requests` rail with an opaque `bookingToken`; the affiliate URL is never held by the client (§16 — no raw outbound). **Lane 1 (UI, this row's first PR):** presence-guarded client contract `PlanCardActivity.affiliateBooking` + pure guard `resolveAffiliateBooking` (fail-closed, §16/§13) + the CTA button, INERT on every item until Lane 2 stamps the field (same posture as primary-action.ts's `book_ride`). **Lane 2 (server, next PR):** `itinerary_items.affiliate_product_id` (nullable, FK SET NULL, omitted from insert schema §19), the resolver rung, and the live-feed reconcile.
